And even things I used to do to feel better, like yoga or walking in the woods, just feel like another task now instead of something I actually want to do.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling more tired or lacking energy lately, even when you’re not doing much?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I mean, I do feel kind of worn out some days, but I haven’t paid close attention to whether it’s actual tiredness or just me not wanting to do things.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel down or depressed most of the day, nearly every day, or does it come and go?\n\nPatient: I’m not completely sure. It’s not like I’ve been tracking it hour by hour. I know I’ve felt pretty low a lot of the time lately, but I can’t say for sure if it’s most of the day nearly every day, or if it kind of shifts.\n\nDoctor: Can you describe how many days out of the week you feel depressed or down, and roughly how much of the day it lasts?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure I can put a number on it. It feels like it’s there a lot of days, but I haven’t really been keeping track. Some days it’s more in the background, and other days it feels like it takes up most of the day.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me if most days feel like the depression is with you most of the day, or does it come and go fairly regularly?\n\nPatient: I’m not totally sure. It feels like it’s there on a lot of days, but I can’t clearly say it’s most of the day every day. Sometimes it comes and goes, and sometimes it just kind of sits there longer.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me if there are specific days when you don’t feel depressed at all, or does it seem to be present every day?\n\nPatient: I honestly don’t know. I can’t really think of a day where I clearly felt fine the whole day, but I also haven’t been paying close enough attention to say it’s definitely every day. It just feels like it’s around a lot.\n\nDoctor: Can you recall if there have been days in the past week when you haven’t felt depressed at all, or has it felt like it’s been with you every day in some form?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I can’t remember a day this past week where I felt completely okay the whole day, but I also don’t want to say for sure it was every single day. It’s just felt like it’s been there in some way most of the time.\n\nDoctor: Looking back over the past two weeks, would you say that on most days, you felt down or depressed for most of the day?\n\nPatient: I think… probably on most days, yes, I felt down at some point. But I’m not completely sure it was most of the day each time. It kind of varies, and I haven’t been tracking it that closely.\n\nDoctor: Over the past two weeks, would you say that on most days, the depressed mood lasted for most of the entire day?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure I can say that clearly. It’s been there a lot, but I haven’t really noticed whether it lasted most of the whole day on most days.\n\nDoctor: Over the past two weeks, on the days when you felt depressed, would you say that the depressed mood was present for most of the entire day?\n\nPatient: I’m honestly not sure. On the days I did feel depressed, it was there for a good part of the day sometimes, but I didn’t really keep track enough to say it was most of the entire day.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me if, on the days when you feel depressed, the low mood tends to last throughout most of the day, or does it come and go more frequently?\n\nPatient: I think it comes and goes some, but when it’s there it can hang around for a while. I just don’t know that I can say it’s most of the day consistently.\n\nDoctor: Over the past two weeks, on the days when you felt depressed, would you say that the low mood was present for most of the entire day?\n\nPatient: I don’t really know. On the days I did feel depressed, it was there for a good part of the day sometimes, but I didn’t really keep track enough to say it was most of the entire day.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me if, on the days when you feel depressed, the low mood tends to last throughout most of the day, or does it come and go more frequently?\n\nPatient: I think it comes and goes some, but when it’s there it can hang around for a while.
